Edit-Preferences-Personal Info
See Also
The Preferences-Personal Info command has two purposes:
 
The Personal Information has two purposes:
-- to designate an item that contains information about you in
the open database.
-- to designate connection definitions that should automatically
be set to your item when adding items to particular
categories.
 
After you fill out this dialog box for the first time, the information
you entered can be viewed and changed using the Preferences-
Personal Info command. If you do not enter the item for yourself
information or if you later delete either the category or item
selected for yourself, the dialog box appears again the next time
you open the database.
 
Setting your personal information is particularly useful if selective
permissions are configured for one or more categories in the
database.
Note: If the active database is a shared database, be sure to
choose a shared category in the Category list box. Shared
categories are indicated by an asterisk at the end of the category
name. By choosing a shared category and item describing
yourself, agents, filters, and other data that use the special (-Me-)
keyword will be properly resolved to the item chosen in this dialog
box, both in your copy of the shared databases and in other
clients' copies. You can select a local category and item, but this
information will not be available to other shared database clients.
 
To use this dialog box:
 
1. Choose the category in which the item describing you exists,
or in which you will create it, from the Category list box. This
is called the user category.
2. Choose the name of the item from the Your Name list box.
This item is called the user item. If the selected category
does not have an item describing you, use the Add New
Item button to display an Add Item dialog box for the
selected category. After you fill out the item detail, select
Save to return to this dialog box.
3. In the large list box, highlight the connection definitions that
you want to automatically point to your item. For example,
highlighting the Activity - Relates to - Employee connection
tells Commence to automatically connect all new Calendar
items to the item you chose in Steps 1 and 2 (you can
override this for individual records on the item detail).
Highlight multiple connection definitions by clicking the mouse
over their names. Remove the highlight from a connection
definition by clicking the mouse over the highlighted name.
4. Select the Save button.
Edit-Preferences-Tool Bars
See Also
Use this command to control how the agent bar, the view bar, and
the status bar are displayed.
 
STYLE
The style you select alters subtle details such as the title bar
colors, window borders, view bar and tool bar background color,
and other visual attributes. Choose None to use the display
settings for your Operating System set via Control Panel.
SHOW AGENT TOOLBAR BUTTON
The agent bar is the row of buttons corresponding to any active
agents that have a Toolbar Pick trigger. If the Show Agent
Toolbar Buttons check box is turned on, the agent bar is
displayed; if not, the agent bar is hidden.
 
SHOW VIEW BAR
If the Show View Bar check box is turned on, the view bar is
displayed; if not, the view bar is hidden.
 
The view bar is the vertical column of icons to the left of the
Commence work area. If you choose to display the view bar, you
can choose to display view icons with or without labels. You can
also choose to display a small or large icons on the view bar. The
view bar can be used by mouse users only.
 
You can have up to 40 view icons on the view bar. The number of
visible view icons that display depend on your display type as well
as your view bar preferences. If your icons don't all fit on the view
bar, Commence will display up/down arrows at the top of the view
bar. You can select these with the mouse to scroll through the
icons.
 
INCLUDE LABELS
Turning off this checkbox will display the view icon but hide the
icon labels; hiding the labels reduces the icon size so the view
bar takes up less space. You can see the view names displayed
on the status bar when you hover your mouse over the icons.
 
USE LARGE BUTTONS
Turning on this checkbox will display full-size icons on the view
bar.
 
To save space for your work area, turn off this checkbox, and
smaller icons will be displayed on the view bar. Small icons allow
you to display more icons (without scrolling).
 
ICON DIRECTORY
You can choose from Commence's internal icons and your own
icons when selecting an icon for a view. You can create your own
icons in files with .ico extensions, and place these files either in a
database directory or in a central icon directory whose location is
set via the Icon Directory text box. The path you specify is
limited to 128 characters.
 
Icons can be created with a variety of drawing applications; you
may also obtain these files from the Internet or other users.
 
SHOW STATUS BAR
If the Show Status Bar check box is turned on, the status bar is
displayed; if not, the status bar is hidden.
 
 
 
 
 
Edit-Preferences-View
See Also
Use the Preferences-View command to set the conditions that
tell Commence what kinds of view
changes should be saved on closing a view, and whether to save
such a view automatically (silently) or display a Save View dialog
box
that allows you to choose a different name under which to save
the changed view.
 
If you are in the habit of having many views open during a
session and closing them when you exit Commence, the
automatic save option can save you some time and keystrokes.
 
 
 
 
Edit-Preferences-Letters
See Also
Use this command to set your preferences for the word
processors that you will use with letters. These preferences will
determine which word processor or text editor you use for text
(unformatted) letters and which word processor you will use for
formatted letters. You also use this command to tell Commence
what directory to use when it creates new letter files.
 
Text letters are created with text letter templates. These are flat
ASCII files that only contain plain text and Field Codes associated
with a particular category in Commence.
 
Formatted letter templates are used to create letters in a
particular word processor's native file format. Formatted
templates are themselves word processor documents and can
contain whatever formatting information the word processor
provides, in addition to the text and Field Codes used in text
templates.
 
If you have WinFax installed on your system and you use one of
the word processors that works with the Commence Letters
facility, then you can set up a new Word Processor Letter
Preference that allows you to fax a letter instead of printing it.
See Letter Fax Macros for more information.
 
The Preferences-Letters command displays the Letter
Preferences dialog box.
View-Open/Manage Views
See Also
Use this command to open, create new, edit and delete a view.
When you select the View-Open/Manage Views command from
the menu bar, Commence displays the Open/Manage Views
dialog box.
The Sort Views By radio group controls the sorting order of the
list box below. You can select one of the radio buttons in the Sort
Views By radio group to sort the list by view name (the default),
view type, or category. Commence remembers your sorting
preference until you exit Commence.
Open View:
To open a view from the list, highlight one of the views and select
the Open button. As a shortcut, double-click the mouse on the
desired view name to open it.
New View:
From the Open/Manage Views dialog box select the Create New
button. Use this command to create a new view by choosing a
category and view type.
Selecting the Create New button displays the New View dialog
box.
To open a new view using the New View dialog box, highlight a
category in the Category list box and select a view type from the
View As list box. If you choose a report view type, choose a
Report Style that includes all the fields from the highlighted
category in the report or the item name field only. Then, select the
View button to create and display the new view.
See View-Open/Manage Views to open a saved view.
Edit View:
To edit a view, highlight a view name from the list box displayed
and select the Edit button. This will display the Edit View dialog
box which allows you to change the name of the saved view;
choose whether or not to display the view's icon on the view bar
for convenient opening; choose an icon and a label for the view;
choose whether or not to display the view's name on the Views
menu for convenient opening.
Delete View:
To delete a view, highlight a view name from the list box
displayed and select the Delete button. As a shortcut, you can
delete a view by double-clicking the mouse on the view name you
want to delete.
Commence displays the Delete View dialog box. A message is
displayed asking for confirmation before deleting the view. Select
Yes to confirm the deletion of the view.
View-Save View
See Also
Use this command to save a new view or to save changes made
to a saved view. This command is available only if there is an
active view.
 
If the active view is a saved view, selecting the View-Save View
command silently saves any changes you have made to the
saved view's options.
 
If the active view is an unsaved view, Commence displays the
Save View dialog box, which asks for a name for the view. View
names are limited to 50 characters.
 
If active view is a saved view, and you want to save the changes
under a new view name (leaving the original saved view
unchanged), use the View-Save View As command.
 
This command does not close the active view. The active view
remains displayed after the completion or cancellation of this
command. (Press CTRL+F4 to close the view.)
 
If you don't save changes made to a (new or saved) view before
closing it or exiting Commence, Commence asks you if you want
to save the changes to the view before closing the view or exiting.
Answering Yes for a saved view silently saves the changes to the
view; for a new view, the Save View dialog box is displayed.
View-Save View As See Also
Use this command to save changes to the active view under a
name other than the name displayed in the title bar of the view's
window (leaving the original saved view unchanged), or to copy a
saved view. This command is available only if there is an active
view.
 
Selecting View-Save View As from the menu bar displays the
Save View dialog box. The name of the active view is filled into
the View Name text box. Type the name under which you want to
save the active view and then select the Save button to save the
view; select the Cancel button to cancel the command.
 
This command does not close the active view. The active view
remains displayed after the completion or cancellation of this
command. (Press CTRL+F4 to close the view.)
 
Use the View-Save View command to save the active view as
itself.
View-Save View As File
 
Use this command to export the data in the active view to a file.
This command is available only if there is an active view in
calendar, report, or grid format. Simply open a saved view (or
create a new view) then select one of the options below.
Create CSV - this auto-creates a simple ASCII text (CSV) file
suitable for importing to another application
Create HTML - this auto-creates a basic HyperText Markup
Language (HTML) file
Create HTML Wizard - select this option if you would like to
apply optional formatting to your HTML file. You will be
prompted step-by-step to select a pre-formatted HTML to use
as a template (for the fonts, colors, layout, etc.), you can add
a header and footer, and also choose the destination folder
and filename where the HTML file will be saved.
You can prepare your view ahead of time using the Customize-
Design View command. Choose only the fields and connections
you wish to include in the file.
Fields: Open the Fields tab to select the fields and
connections to display. Only these columns will be included
in the saved file.
Field Labels: If you have custom field labels for each column,
these custom labels will be used as the column headers.
Filters: If you have filters on the view, these are supported.
Only the items displayed in the view will be included in the
file.
Sorts: The file will be sorted the same as your view.
Group Headers and Group Totals: Grouping is not supported;
these will be skipped.
Fonts and Color codes: Font formatting is not applied when
using the Create CSV or Create HTML commands. To create
a formatted HTML, use the Create HTML Wizard command
and select your custom HTML template to apply these fonts
and colors to your saved HTML file.
Create HTML Wizard
Perform the following steps to create an HTML file using the
wizard:
 
1. Open a calendar, report or grid view.
If the view is not currently in calendar, report or grid format,
use the View-View As command to change the view type to
either calendar, report or grid.
2. Select the View-Save View As File- Create HTML Wizard
command from the ribbon to save the view to an external
HTML file. The Save As HTML dialog box appears.
3. Follow the on-screen instructions to create an HTML file with
data from the active view.
The resulting file can then be viewed using a standard Web
browser, such as Microsoft Internet Explorer.
 
Selecting the Cancel button will cancel the command and will not
save a file.
 
The Save View as File commands do not modify the active view.
 
View-View As
See Also
Use this command to display the active view as a new view using
a different view type.
 
The View-View As group of commands effectively copies the
active view to a new view type. Select any view type (Report,
Book, Calendar, Gantt, Grid, Report Viewer) from the menu bar.
 
A new view is opened using the (possibly filtered) active category
and the new view type specified. The previous active view (on
which the new view is based) remains displayed in the
background and is left unchanged.
 
If you want to retain this new view, you must save it using the
View-Save View or View-Save View As commands.
Switch Windows-1, 2, 3...
See Also
Make the selected view the active view. Choosing an open view
name from the Window menu makes it easy for you to switch to
another view when you have a number of views displayed and
some are hidden behind others.
 
Note that you can also use the key combination CTRL+F6 to
successively select views on the desktop as the active view.
 
A numbered list of open views appears at the bottom of the View
menu. The text of the entry on the menu for a view matches the
view title shown in the title bar of the view window.
 
The currently active view has a check mark next to its name in
the list. Selecting a view from the list makes the chosen view the
active view.
View-Cascade
Use this command to layer all open windows on the desktop, so
that the title bar of each is visible. This makes it easier for you to
switch between open views, while displaying as much of the
active view as possible.
View-Tile Horizontally
Use this command to size and re-display all open views
horizontally on the desktop, so that no window overlaps another.
This lets you see at least a portion of all the currently open views.
View-Tile Vertically
Use this command to size and re-display all open views vertically
on the desktop, so that no window overlaps another. This lets you
see at least a portion of all the currently open views.
View-Resize All
Resize all open views to fit on the active desktop while
maintaining the relative proportions and positions of the individual
views.
View-Close All See Also
Close all open windows on the desktop (including any hidden
behind other windows). This command provides an alternative to
closing each individual window.
 
If any of the open views have been changed and the changes not
saved, you will be prompted to save the views, depending on
your view preference settings.
View-More Windows
See Also
Displays the Select Window dialog box. This dialog box lets you
select a view other than one of the nine shown on the Window
menu to be the active view. Only nine open views can appear on
the Window menu. If more than nine views are open, the More
Windows... command is placed on the Window menu. Choosing
an open view name from the Window menu makes it easy for you
to switch to another view when you have many views displayed
and some are hidden behind others.
 
Note that you can also use the key combination CTRL+F6 to
successively select views in the workspace as the active view.
 
The currently active view has a check mark next to its name in
the list at the bottom of the Window menu. If the active view does
not appear on the Window menu, a check mark appears next to
the More Windows... command.

See Also
Use this command or the corresponding button on the tool bar to
display and edit the design for the active view or displayed item
detail.
Display Specify a default title and paper layout for printing a view.
Fields Select the fields to be displayed on the view, and decide
what order to display them in. Combine multiple
fields to be displayed on the same line. Change the
font used for each field individually.
Colors Select the color code template to be used for the view.
Turn on options to show legend or color code item
text.
Fonts Select the font and font size used for item text and label
text.
Report Viewer
When you select the Customize-Design View command with an
active Report Viewer window, the following options are displayed.
Filter/Sort Select the filter and sort for the report viewer.
Designer Launch the Report Designer for the active report viewer
window.
Detail Form
When you select the Customize-Design View command with an
active item detail, the Design Detail Form dialog box is displayed.
 
Note: The changes you make to views using the Customize
menu commands are temporary unless you save the view; see
View-Save View and View-Save View As.
Customize-Filter View Use this command to select a
subset of items from the active category for display
by the active view. This subset can be based on up
to four field values, connected item names, and/or
connected field values. You can also press CTRL+F
or select the corresponding filter button from the
tool bar to invoke this command.
 
The Customize-Filter View command displays the Filters dialog
box.
 
The Clear All button on the Summary tab clears all filters and
displays all items in the category.
Customize-Sort View (Gantt, Report and Grid view
types only)
Use this command, or the corresponding button on the tool bar, to
change the sort fields for the items in a Gantt chart, report or grid
view. This command is available only if the active view is a Gantt
chart, report or grid view.
 
Items in a report and grid views, by default, are sorted in
ascending ASCII order by the name field of each item.
 
Items in a Gantt chart view, by default, are sorted by three fields:
 
First, by the Start Date field, so that items with the earliest
start dates are displayed at the top of the chart, the latest at
the bottom.
Second, by the End Date field, so that items with the same
start date are displayed in ascending date order within the
group of items with the same start date.
Third, by the item name field, so that items with the same start
and end dates are displayed in ascending ASCII order by their
item name fields.
 
(Note that the fields used for Start Date and End Date in a Gantt
chart view are set when the view is created; you can change them
using the Customize-Design View command.)
 
Using the Customize-Sort View command, you can change:
 
the number of sort fields
the fields used to sort items (maximum of four fields)
the sort type for each sort field (ascending or descending
ASCII)
the groups and sub-totals (for report and grid views only)
 
Selecting the Customize-Sort View command displays the Sort
Fields dialog box.
Customize-Desktops
A desktop is a group of open views. You may want to define one
desktop for your Project Management reports and another for
your Contact Management views. A database can have up to 100
desktops defined. You can display desktops as stacked tabs on
the Commence view bar.
 
To open a desktop: Select Customize-Open/Manage Desktop.
From there you can highlight the desktop that you would
like to open and select the Open button. If you have the
desktop displayed as a tab, you only need to click on the
tab for that desktop.
 
To display the Desktop Tabs:
Select Customize-Open/Manage Desktop. Check the
Show As View Bar Tabs check box.
 
To Add a New Desktop:
Select Customize-Open/Manage Desktop Select the Add
button. Choose a name for your new desktop. This name
should describe the type of information that you will
display on this tab.
If you mark the Save Desktop on Exit checkbox, the
desktop will be saved each time you exit Commence.
If you mark the Show As View Bar Tab checkbox, a tab
will be displayed the Commence View Bar. If you do not
show the desktop tab, you will be able to access the
desktop by selecting Customize-Open/Manage Desktop
You can also choose the color the desktop tab and you
can choose a bitmap (*.BMP) or JPEG file to use as the
background of the desktop.
 
To Edit or Delete a Desktop:
Select Customize-Open/Manage Desktop. From there
you can highlight the desktop that you would like to edit or
delete, select the appropriate button.
Customize-Database
One of the most powerful features of Commence is its flexibility.
You can add and change categories, connections between
categories, create agents, etc. to suit your specific needs.
For more information about a particular topic, select the topic
from the list below:
Agent Add, edit, copy, or delete agents in the active database.
Category Add, edit, copy, or delete categories in the active
database.
Color Code Add, edit, and delete color code templates in the
active database.
Connection Add, rename, and delete connection definitions in the
active database.
Detail Form Add, edit, copy, and delete item detail forms for the
selected category in the active database.
Import/Export Add, edit, copy, and delete import/export templates
in the active database.
Letter Template Add, edit, and delete letter templates for the
selected category in the active database.

Tools-Outlook Sync
See Also
Integrating Commence with Microsoft Outlook enables
Commence users to share their meetings, contacts, and tasks/to-
dos with Outlook users. The result is a comprehensive
calendar,address book, and Task list in both applications.
 
To synchronize information between Commence and Outlook:
1 Start Commence and open the database that is configured to
synchronize with Microsoft Outlook.
2 In Commence, select Tools - Outlook Sync.
3 A dialog will be displayed showing the status of the
synchronization between Commence and Outlook.
Note: This command will synchronize all mapped Outlook
applications at the same time.
Tools-Spell Check
Use this command to check the spelling of words in a text field;
either the cursor or some highlighted text must be present in the
text field in order to start the spell checker. Otherwise, the Tools-
Spell Check command is unavailable.
 
If spellings appear in the text that are not in Commence's
dictionary, or your personal dictionary, Commence displays the
Spell Checker dialog box.
 
You can:
 
accept single or multiple occurrences of a misspelled word
edit the misspelled word by typing a correction from the
keyboard or choosing a correction from a list of suggestions
generated by the spell checker
add spellings to your personal dictionary
 
Select a button from the dialog box to perform the indicated
operation on the currently highlighted word or select Done to exit
the spell checker.
 
If the spell checker finds no spelling mistakes in the field text,
Commence displays a message box that informs you of this fact.
Select OK from the box and the spell checker exits.
Tools-Field Summary
See Also
Use the Tools-Field Summary command to display a total and/or
average of the selected calculation and number fields for the
items in the view. For example, the field summary can be used to
calculate total sales for a month, or to check if expenses have
exceeded a maximum amount.
 
The summary takes the data in the selected fields for all the items
in the active view, and shows the name of each field followed by
one or both of:
 
the combined arithmetic average of the data in the field for
all items
 
the combined total of the data in the field for all items
 
The summary for the selected fields will appear in a Field
Summary dialog box. The field summary also appears at the
bottom of the printed report. This command is available only if the
active view is a report.
 
The fields shown in the summary report (and whether to calculate
the average, total, or both) are selected using the Customize-
Design View command. Select the Fields Tab. Move (append or
insert) the field into the report and select the Field Options for
that field. Check the boxes labeled Total or Average (one or both
boxes may be checked as needed).
 
Note: Only the calculation and number fields displayed in the view
and whose field options have been set for summary reporting
appear in the field summary.
Tools-Schedule Meeting
If you are scheduling appointments for a group of people, you can
use this command to find a date and time for a meeting based
upon the availability of selected participants. Once you’ve
selected a date and time, the item is added to your group
calendar. Other meeting details (location, comments, resources,
and a meeting coordinator) can also be chosen. Once you
schedule the meeting, you can review the meeting's details by
opening a view of the Appointment category or by selecting the
item detail from your calendar.
 
When you select the Tools-Schedule Meeting command, the
Schedule Meeting dialog box appears.
 
If Microsoft Outlook Calendar is configured in the Edit-
Preferences-Other Apps menu command, the Schedule Meeting
command will launch the Microsoft Outlook Meeting Scheduler.
Refer to the Microsoft Outlook online help for additional
information about using the Microsoft Outlook Meeting Scheduler.
 
Note: For information about using the Schedule Meeting
command with custom categories and connection definitions, see
Meeting Preferences command.

Tools-Sync Link Out
See Also
Use this command to:
Define the remote PCs that will be in your sync link group.
Edit or delete the remote PCs in your sync link group.
Create an initial update file for each of the remote databases.
Create an incremental update of your database to take a
remote database.
Selecting the Sync Link Out command displays the Sync Link
Out Options dialog box.
 
In this dialog box, you can define and maintain a set of two or
more identical databases (called a sync link group). You can also
manage this sync link group by adding, editing, and deleting
remote databases. Once you establish a sync link group you can
compress a Commence database into a single file (called an
update file) to sync link the database changes from one PC to
the other as you move between them. When you choose the
database to synchronize and select OK, the sync out begins.
Tools-Transfer In
See Also
Use this command to copy the definitions (and data, if any)
contained in a file previously created with the Transfer Out
command.
Overview
Selecting the File-Transfer In command displays the Select
Transfer File dialog box. Specify the file name of the transfer file
and If the selected transfer file contains only definitions and no
data, the transfer in process begins when you select OK.
 
Data definitions are always added unless there is an existing
definition of the same type with the same name (e.g., you try to
transfer in a People category into a database that already has a
People category). If Transfer In detects a definition in the
transfer file with the same name as an already existing definition
in the open database, the incoming definition is not transferred.
See the Transferring In Definitions with Duplicate Names section
in the description of the Transfer Rules dialog box for information
about using Transfer In to merge similar but different definitions
with the same name.
 
Note: Data items may have been included in a transfer file
explicitly as part of the transfer out operation, or included
automatically by Commence because a category, view, or agent
definition being transferred depends on them. See Transfer Out
for more information on items included in a transfer file.
.
Tools -Transfer Out
See Also
Transfer Out copies selected categories, views, and agents from
a Commence database into a DOS file. You may optionally
choose to copy the data contained in selected categories and
views. The resulting transfer file is the input file for a Transfer In
command.
 
Use this command to:
Move definitions you have developed in one database into
another of your own databases.
Share definitions you have developed with another
Commence user.
Overview
Selecting the Transfer Out command displays the Transfer Out
Options dialog box.
 
In this dialog box, you specify whether to transfer all definitions
for selected categories or just the definitions required by
selected views of the categories. Then you select any agents to
transfer. You also select whether to transfer items and related
data files (DOS files pointed to by data file fields). Once you've
made your choices and selected OK, then specify a transfer file
name. Once you choose a file name, the transfer out begins.
 
Automatic Inclusion of Dependent Data and
Definitions
When you select categories, views, or agents for transfer,
Commence automatically determines the additional definitions
and data that are required by a category or view, or those
required for the operation of an agent. Dependent data for a
category or view might be names of connected items in other
categories or color code templates. Dependent data for an agent
might be another agent or items used by a filter in a trigger,
condition, or action.
 
The following list shows the dependent data and definitions
selected for categories, views, and agents:
 
Category All category field definitions.
All category connection definitions and all directly related
definitions from the connected category.
All item detail forms for the category.
All view definitions that use the transferred category, their
options and filters.
All color code templates for the category.
View Associated category's definition, along with the categories
of any indirect fields.
All connection definitions used in the view.
All item detail forms for the relevant categories.
All color code templates usable by the view.
Agent All necessary categories, views, and other agents needed
by the agent.
All data items from relevant categories used in trigger, condition,
or action filters.
Note: If you want to transfer only items, use the Export-By
Category command or the Export By-View command to create a
DOS file and use the Import command to read the file into a
database.
 
 
Specifying Data File Transfer Location
The File-Transfer Out command lets you transfer out items as
well as definitions to a DOS file. If you elect to transfer out items,
you may also choose to transfer out any related data files (i.e.,
DOS files pointed to by data file type fields) that are referenced
by the transferred items.
 
When you transfer into a database a file that has definitions,
items, and related data files, Commence needs to know where to
put the related data files on your hard disk. This is because the
directory structure on your computer and the directory structure
of the computer on which the transfer file was created are most
likely different.
 
Select the Edit-Preferences-Other command to choose a location
in which to store related data files transferred into the currently
open database. Enter the directory name and overwrite option in
the External Data Files group box.
Tools-Import
See Also
Use this command to populate a category in your open
Commence database using:
data from external applications or
data exported from another category in this or another
Commence database
To import category data, highlight the name of the import/export
template you want to use and select the Import button.
 
When you select the Import button, Commence displays the
Select Import File dialog box so you can select the name of the
external file you want to import.
 
Commence scans the import file you choose and displays a
message box if it finds a problem with the file, such as incorrect
formatting. The message displayed indicates the source of the
problem. Select OK to return to the Import dialog box; to
continue importing, you must either fix the import file or pick
another one to import.
 
Provided the file you chose matches the format of your
import/export template, and no errors are found in the file,
Commence begins importing the data in the external file into the
category in your open Commence database. Commence displays
a message box as the import progresses. You can select Cancel
to stop the import operation, but this will leave the import partially
completed and your category contents in an indeterminate state.
When the import is complete, select OK.
Tools-Export-By View
See Also
Use this command to copy data from items in the active view to
an external file
for use with external applications or
for use in another category, either in the open database or in
a different Commence database
Only the items that appear in the view are included in the export.
 
Use the Export By View command with a filtered view to export
data from a subset of the items in a category. If you use the
Archive option when you export, only the items in the view will be
deleted from the category; this is a handy way of backing up old
items and deleting them from the category to make room for new
items.
 
The dialog boxes displayed by and the behavior of this command
are identical to the Export-By Category command, except that
the items exported are a subset of the items in the category as
determined by the view filter. If the view is unfiltered, then all
items from the active category are exported.
 
Selecting this command displays the Export dialog box.
Tools-Export-By Category
See Also
Use this command to copy data in a category to an external file:
for use with external applications or
for use in another category, either in the open database or in
a different Commence database
You can export data that is in one of three external file formats,
ASCII, dBASE, or DIF.
 
You tell Commence how to export to an external data file by
defining an import/export template. This template tells Commence
how to extract data from your category and how to write your
external file. If you use the Archive option when you export by
category, all the items in the category will be deleted from the
database; this is a handy way of backing up old items and
deleting them from the category to make room for new items.
 
The Export-By Category command displays the Export dialog
box.

Help-Tutorial Database
 
To enter the tutorial environment, select the Tutorial Database
button on the Help ribbon menu. This creates a special 'sand-box'
environment in which you can practice using Commence without
affecting any of your live data in any other database. Note this is not
a self-running demo; you can try out any command or feature at
your own pace.
 
The Help-Tutorial Database command:
 
changes to an alternate database directory used only for
tutorial data. Select the Help-System Information-Directory
Information command for the path to the tutorial database.
optionally re-populates the tutorial database with a fresh copy
of the sample data
highlights the Tutorial Database button on the Help ribbon
menu, to remind you that you are currently in the tutorial
environment
disables the Open/Manage Database command, so that you
must exit the tutorial database using the Help-Tutorial
Database command before opening another database
disables the Sync Link In, and Sync Link Out commands;
these commands are unavailable while in the Tutorial
 
Each time you select this command, you are asked whether you
want to replace the existing tutorial data with a fresh copy of the
data. If you select Yes, the data currently in the tutorial database is
overwritten with a new copy of the data as it first appeared when
you installed Commence. Any changes you've made to the tutorial
data are lost. This is a convenient way of starting with fresh tutorial
data after you experiment with it for a while. Selecting No retains the
current data in the tutorial database so that you can return to the
environment as you left it.
 
Once you change to the tutorial data, the title bar of the Commence
main window changes to indicate you are using tutorial data.
 
To return to your live database, select the Help-Tutorial Database
menu entry again; or, exit Commence and then restart it.
 
Some of the examples in the help system refer to the tutorial data.
You may want to switch to using the Tutorial data as you read
certain sections of help.

Introduction
See Also
Commence Corporation offers different software products which
let you operate in either standalone
or workgroup
environments.
In a standalone installation the Commence software is used to
maintain databases to which only one user at a time from a single
PC can have access.
This version of Commence is referred to as Commence
standalone. See Basic Concepts for an introduction to basic
Commence features.
In a typical workgroup environment, several Commence users on
different PCs with access to a shared FTP directory, a local area
network (LAN) or E-Mail may share database information using
the Commence Server and Client software. This version of
Commence is referred to as Commence Workgroup. Commence
Workgroup’s networking capabilities allow users to share and
periodically synchronize a database with the server using either a
local area network or a compatible Windows e-mail program.
Users on different PCs can share information in the same
databases, viewing and updating information simultaneously.
They may also define private information which other users
cannot see or access. Different levels of authorization can be
defined to control various access privileges.
Commence uses a distributed database architecture where each
client
of the workgroup has their own local copy of the shared
database. This has many advantages, including:
 
Users
can use their local copy of the shared database off-line.
While connected to the LAN, data is automatically
synchronized with all other users on the network. When
disconnected, Commence remembers the changes that have
been made. Then when remote users log back into the
network, by physically re-attaching or by calling in from a
remote client PC, their changes and updates are
automatically synchronized with the other users, and vice
versa, thereby keeping everyone’s' data in sync. This has the
added advantage of allowing users to continue using
Commence when the network is down. Those who
synchronize via e-mail will continue to communicate their
changes to the server as long as they can send and receive
e-mail.
. Similarly, users who synchronize using an Internet
connection will continue synchronizing as long as the Internet
connection is intact.
There
is no data locking so all information is available at all
times, authorizations permitting. Some other workgroup
products lock records as they are being viewed making them
unavailable to other users. Your data is in a local database
and is always available.
Users
can create private information that stays at their local
copy of the shared database. Private or local information
does not synchronize with the server or with any other users
of the workgroup, thereby protecting the user’s private
information.
One copy of a shared database resides on the Commence server,
which
is created when you, the Administrator, install the Server Edition
of Commence. Each Commence user has his or her own copy of
Commence, called the Commence client, and a copy of the
shared database, called the client database. Together the server
and Commence clients, along with their copies of the shared
database, are called a Commence workgroup.
 
Typical Commence Workgroup Configuration
With this model, authorized workgroup users always have instant
access to information in the shared database. All additions, edits,
etc., that a user makes to the shared data at his or her client are
sent via the LAN to the server copy of the database, and changes
made by other workgroup users are sent to each user's client
database. Even if the user disconnects the client PC from the
LAN and make changes to the data, the changes are
automatically sent to the server database when the user
reconnects to the LAN. In other words, the workgroup server and
its clients are constantly synchronized, and all workgroup users
have the most recently updated information at all times.
 
 
 
 
Getting Started
There are several things that you must do to install and set up
your Commence workgroup. These steps should be performed in
the following order.
1. Install the server software. You need to run the installation
program and determine the proper paths for the various
components of the Commence Server.
2. Administer licenses. Before a user can be added to the
Commence Administration Program, ComAdmin, you must
first set the total number of users in your workgroup using
the License Administration program.
3. Add users. Users of a Commence Workgroup need to be
added to ComAdmin. A user must be added to be able to
use a shared database and share information.
4. Set up a shared database. You must define a shared
database. This will be the basis for your Commence
workgroup. You set up the shared database in ComAdmin.
5. Start the server. To do any of the following things, the server
must be running in the shared database defined in the
previous step.
6. Set permissions. Commence Workgroup Information
Manager has many different permissions that you can set
per database and per user to restrict what they can see and
what they can do in the shared database. Commence will
use the default values unless you change them.
7. Set communications settings. Each user of Commence can
synchronize their data with the server using either a shared
LAN directory or e-mail. You must tell the server what
method each client will be using.
8. Register clients with the database. You must tell the server
what users are to be clients of the shared database. This will
create a link between the clients and the server.
9. Install the client software. Each client can install the client
software themselves, but you must make sure it is properly
installed. If it is not, they may have problems with their
Commence Workgroup.
10. Enroll to the shared database. Enrolling to a shared
database will bring a copy of the shared database locally to
the client. This process involves running the Enroll Wizard,
entering a special authorization key, and some
communication parameters.
What's New in Commence RM?
Commence RM is designed with two objectives in mind. First, to
ensure that the software remains compatible with changes to
current versions of Microsoft Office and Windows Operating
Systems. These changes are not always visible to the end user,
but are critical to protect the investment and usability of the
software.
Secondly is to enhance the functionality and utilization of the
software. These modifications are traditionally driven by our global
customer base who periodically interacts with our product
management staff and makes recommendations for future
enhancements. We appreciate their initiative and invite all
customers to reach out to us with any recommendations you may
have for future enhancements.
The new features are summarized below.
 
 
Commence Email Dropbox
One of the core enhancements in RM 8.0 provides better integration
with Microsoft Office 365 and Google Mail for logging of e-mails. A
new Email Dropbox feature allows users to log emails by simply
forwarding them to a configured email address, such as
crm@mycompany.com. Commence can be configured to check this
inbox and automatically download and log any emails that are
received.
 
Report writer Improvements for Adobe PDF
Use the built-in Commence Report writer to create interactive forms
in PDF format.
Use the full power of the report designer to publish pre-configured
and ready to fill AcroForms.
 
Customize Database confirms Updates and Deletes
This improvement notifies the Commence administrator when
making changes or deletions of the Application design, including
views, agents, fields, and more.
For example, when removing a field from the application, Commence
shows an alert and documents any dependencies such as views or
agents that use this field. This makes it safer and easier to update
and modernize your application without causing unintended
changes.
 
Image fields support more image formats
The Commence application database provides the Image field and
allows for the application to store images with each record, such as a
contact’s profile picture or a picture of a real estate listing. This
image field has been enhanced to support most common image
formats such as JPEG, GIF and more.
 
Agent documentation in System info
Provides an improved System Info screen including an Agents listing
that can be exported to a file for review or printing.
 
Social Integration
Integration with popular social search sites will allow the user to
search for contact information on sites like Twitter, LinkedIn and
Facebook. This feature provides the ability to add social links within
Commence RM and navigate to a social user’s profile.
 
Commence Mobilelink Add-on
Want to synchronize your calendar and contacts from Commence to
your Smartphone?
Well now you can. Commence includes our Mobilelink software, a
plugin software component that synchronizes data with MS Outlook
and Google.
 
Office 2016 Styles
Commence RM includes new styles to modernize the application
look and feel.
 
Report Writer
The built-in report writer has been updated to include the latest
Active Reports features, allowing you to create more informative
and analytical types of reports as required by your business.
Of course all your existing reports will continue to be supported,
preserving your investment in your current application reports. Now
supports creating reports including charts, tables, calendars, and
mapping.
 
Integration with Cloud Based File Storage and Sharing
Systems
Commence applications often include attached files, for example
logging letters, logging emails, attached project and sales
documents, and many other purposes. While Commence
Workgroup includes file sharing features that allow attached files
to be shared among clients, cloud-based file sharing services offer
enhanced functionality and easier access from anywhere and from
any device. Commence RM will allow these cloud-based files to
be used as file attachments in your Commence application.
This means that you can store and access files directly in the cloud
instead of storing them locally on a desktop computer, making it
easier to share files with other users and access them from multiple
devices.
 
Integration with Third Party Marketing Programs
The Commence RM software includes email marketing features
that allows you to send bulk emails to your customers and
contacts using the “Connect for Email” application and your own
email server.
In RM 6.1, we have extended this capability and will support
integration with popular cloud based e-mail marketing services such
as MailChimp and Constant Contact. The Connect for Email
application will enable you to upload your contact list from
Commence RM and send bulk emails through these email services.
In addition, you will be able to access email marketing reports
directly from within the Connect for Email application. Common
reports include details about campaigns that were sent, which emails
were opened, and which were deleted allowing you to better
understand the effectiveness of your email marketing campaigns.
 
Microsoft Office 64-bit support
Commence RM includes support for 64-bit editions of Microsoft
Office, including top used features such as mail merge letters and
email logging. With support for both 32-bit and 64-bit Office
versions, Commence allows you to use the Office version you
require, removing any extra implementation work to install 32-bit
Office versions.
 
Macros for unsupported word processors have also been removed
from the installer.
 
Spell Checker
The built-in Spell Check feature has been improved to work with
64-bit versions of Windows, as requested by many of our
customers. The feature has been rebuilt using a modern spell
check engine that also includes support for multiple languages
and dictionaries.
 
Modern Tabbed Dialog Boxes
The most frequently used dialog box screens in Commence have
been updated with a modern look and feel, making them easier to
navigate and use. In addition, these new screens can be resized
allowing more information to be displayed on larger desktop
screens.
 
Save View as File
The Save View as HTML command has been replaced with a
more versatile Save View as File command. A quick export is now
available by choosing either Create CSV or Create HTML. The
Create CSV command instantly saves a comma separated (CSV)
file format that can be imported directly to Microsoft Excel or
uploaded to online web services, such as email marketing
services. Similarly, the Create HTML command saves your view
with a single click to HTML format. The formatted HTML option is
still available using the Create HTML Wizard command.
 
 
Redesigned Filter Summary
Setting the search criteria for reports and views is easier than ever
using the new Filters window. The summary page now shows all 8
filters on a single page. Filters can also be drag and dropped from
positions 1-4 to 5-8 as per earlier designs. This allows you to quickly
move filters or clone filters as needed to change the Boolean logic of
your query.
 
 
Security Improvements
Security has been improved by incorporating the latest secure
features for email and FTP.
 
How to get the new version
 
Commence RM is a Windows desktop version that can be
installed on premises or in a web-hosted environment. Customers
with an Internet connection may download the update directly
using the Check for Updates command found on the Help menu.
The online update feature is a service available at no additional
cost to customers on a current maintenance plan. Installation CDs
are available upon request.
 
 
 
 
 
 
Includes Features from Previous Releases
This release includes all the new features and enhancements to
the product's user interface introduced in Commence RM 3.5 , RM
3.5.1, RM 3.6, RM 4.0, RM 4.1, and RM 5.0. A summary of the
feature highlights are listed below. Contact Technical Support for
more detailed information.
 
Commence RM 5.0
Database Capacities
Database capacity increase to allow up to 250 fields per
category (increased from 100 fields).
View icon labels allow 50 characters (increased from 10
characters)
Calculation field changes to support the new database
format
Database Backup tool updated to support data folders
larger than 2Gb
Resolved progress bar issues with Database Restore tool
for backup (*.xfr) files larger than 1Gb
Microsoft Office 365 and 2013
Installer improvements to better support Word macro
installation for Office 365
Resolved issues with Excel charting feature in Office 2013
Improved Word Mail Merge macros to better support
merging special character in connected item fields (i.e.,
large text field with carriage returns)
Report Writer
Updated to latest version of Active Reports
Conversion utility has been improved to handle
mismatched fields when upgrading existing report viewers.
Resolved error in creating new report viewer with all fields
in the category.
Unconverted reports can continue to be used indefinitely.
Grid Views & Multi-views
Resolved grid view focus issue when a detail form is
closed; highlight no longer moves to first record.
Grid now supports copy function (CTRL + C) to clipboard.
Resolved multi-view focus issue; no longer requires click
to begin using prefix matching.
Miscellaneous
Outlook Connect Email Add-In updated to use newer
version of Visual Studio
Mobile Link Add-In updated to better handle trivial updates
to Notes during synchronization with Outlook.
Increased default size for ACTIVE.LOG to capture more
activity.
Resolved read-only file permissions issue with
COMMENCE.INI on Windows 7 (64-bit).
Improved performance of Delete All Items command.
Commence RM 4.1
Microsoft Office 2013 and Windows 8
Windows 8 installations supported
Updated email integration for logging email in Outlook
2013 (32 bit)
Office 2013 (32 bit) supported for Word mail merges and
other integration features.
Report Writer
New report designer interface including, new toolbar, new
script editor and preview tabs, and toolbox properties.
New chart control to insert bar, pie and line charts
New report viewer features including Continuous scrolling
and Annotation
Conversion utility to easily update existing report viewers
to the new format. Unconverted reports can continue to be
used indefinitely.
Grid View
Redesigned grid view for improved performance and
user interface.
New tool tip pop-up to improve visibility of truncated cells.
Reposition columns on-the-fly with simple drag and drop.
Workgroup Expert Report
Client sync status summary chart appears before detailed
recommendation text.
Client workgroup ID numbers appear in workgroup
summary and client sync status charts for easier
troubleshooting.
Installation
Default installation for 'All Users' no longer requires
Windows Administrator rights.
Program settings previously saved to the Windows
Registry (HKLM) are moved to COMMENCE.INI file.
'Current User' installations are unchanged and
continue to be saved in the Windows Registry
(HKCU).
Database settings previously saved to the Windows
Registry (HKLM) are moved to DATABASE.INI file.
Outlook Add-in updated to read/write to INI file instead of
the Windows Registry.
Miscellaneous
Larger windows for Open Manage Views, Send Letter, and
Workgroup Assign Users dialog boxes
Ribbon and Quick access toolbar improvement for Agents
Graphical charts can now be added to the Dashboard.
Resolved window focus issues during Dashboard refresh.
Commence RM 4.0
Microsoft Office 2010 and Windows 7
Windows 7 installations supported
Ongoing development and improvements to support 64-bit
operating systems. Commence installs and runs as a 32-
bit application in 64-bit Windows OS.
Updated email integration for Outlook 2007 and Outlook
2010.
Office 2010 (Word, Excel) supported for the various
integration features, including mail merges and Excel
graphs of sales opportunity data.
Ribbon
All new Ribbon replaces the Menu bar. Note: For ease of
explaining the new features, the terms ribbon and menu
will be used interchangeably.
Commence Application button intuitively presents
application-related commands to manage the active
database, open a different database, or exit the
application.
Ribbon can be minimized to increase workspace area.
Opens when clicked, collapses when not in use.
New Aero-style icons for menu commands that were
previously not featured prominently, such as Outlook sync
and Schedule Meeting.
Quick Access Toolbar
Customizable Quick access toolbar is provided for all your
frequently used commands.
Always on top for anytime/anywhere access.
Each user in a workgroup can individually personalize
his/her quick access toolbar.
Right-click on any object in the ribbon, then choose 'Add
to Quick Access Toolbar'
Launch menu commands, toolbar agents, menu agents,
even views!
Everything you need in the smallest space possible.
Minimize the ribbon and work entirely off the quick access
toolbar.
Key Tips
With the elimination of cascading menus, most commands
are now accessible with fewer keystrokes.
Hold the ALT key, press the letter for the menu tab, then
press the letters shown in the Key Tip over the feature that
you want to use.
Backwards compatibility for common keyboard shortcuts
from prior versions of Commence (e.g. CTRL+P still Prints
the active view).
In-Ribbon Galleries
Home menu is visible as soon as the application opens,
and displays a gallery of graphical icons for each
command.
Built-in command toolbar and user-defined agent toolbar
reside on the Home menu.
Complementary features have been grouped together. For
example: Global Search, Find, Find & Replace are now
found in the Search group on the Edit menu.
Commands to manage views (open, save, view as) and
arrange view windows (tile, cascade) have been
consolidated into a single core tab -- the View menu.
Enhanced tooltips appear when hovering over a gallery
icon. Tooltips briefly explain the command and give the
shortcut keys.
Visit the Reference book of this online help for a handy
chart of all menu commands and keyboard shortcuts.
Dashboard
Dashboard page provides an overview of your key
Commence information similar to Outlook Today.
Ready-to-use portals display your Calendar, Contacts, and
Task list from Commence
Contacts list also serves as an index to quickly access the
selected contact's profile on LinkedIn and Twitter.
Ability to create your own portals to display data from your
custom Commence category
Integrates with your MAPI mail client configured in
Preferences to display the number of messages in the
Inbox, Outbox, and Drafts folders.
Dashboard can be viewed as (1) a separate document
view within Commence, (2) a secondary tab within the
Contacts multiview, or (3) an external web page running
outside of Commence.
Social Networking
Social Networking page of the Dashboard features three
integrated portals: Contacts list, Contact Details window,
and Social Network window.
Selecting a Contact in the list displays that contact's
details in the second window.
In the Contact Details window, three new icons are used to
interact with the social networking sites. Clicking on any of
the three populates the corresponding social network
details in the third window.
Twitter button displays the selected Contact's Twitter
profile. Tweets button displays recent messages left by the
individual on their Twitter page.
Google and Smart Phone synchronization
Mobile Link features direct synchronization with Google's
web-based applications for managing Calendar
appointments, Contacts, and Tasks.
Enables Web browser access to your calendar, contacts,
and tasks from anywhere you have Internet access
Use any web-enabled device to access your Commence
data via Google, including desktops, smart phones, and
even the latest Apple iPad.
Mobile Link also supports two-way syncs with most of the
smart phones available today, including iPhone, iPad,
BlackBerry, Android, Palm WebOS (Pre and Pixi),
Windows Mobile, and Symbian S60 devices.
Workgroup synchronization
Monitor your workgroup client right at the desktop level
using enhanced Help menu features.
Help-System Information command display additional
Workgroup information such as date of last sync, exact
number of changes behind, and specific sync points.
Message Log Info button, found on the Home menu and
also the Help menu, displays up to the minute alerts on
the Ribbon
White Message Log icon indicates a normal state and let's
you know with confidence that you have received all
updates from the Commence Server database.
Yellow, Orange, and Red icons indicate varying degrees of
alert conditions, i.e. how far behind or how long it's been
since your last synchronization.
Warning thresholds can be adjusted to meet your
company's requirements, alerting you based on a certain
number of days or hours, or a certain number of changes.
Outlook Email Logging
Commence add-in updates include better support for
Microsoft Exchange, Outlook 2007 and Outlook 2010.
New Aero-style Log button for the Add-ins menu better
integrates with the Outlook Ribbon for a more seamless
look.
Calendar
Week numbers are a new feature prominently displayed
for each week in the mini-month calendars for Day, 2 Day,
Week, 2 Weeks, Month, and 6 Weeks format.
'All Day Events' and 'Untimed' appointments are now
displayed in their own row when using the Send command
on the Home menu to send you calendar to an email
recipient.
Corrected calibration issues with using Right click on a
calendar to add a new appointment on the selected date.
Repaired minor issue in Calendar views which listed the
incorrect calendar display mode (2 Day) when actually
viewing Day mode.
Improved Calendar printing to better handle monthly
calendar views that span six weeks
Mini-month dates are shown in bold text when
appointments are added to that date.
Miscellaneous
Continued support for custom high-resolution icons when
designing Agent toolbar shortcuts for the Ribbon and
Quick access toolbar.
Added support for colon (:) character in view names when
using the Send command on the Home menu to send a
view to an email recipient.
Commence RM 3.6
New Styles and Icons
Personalize your view bar, tool bar, and desktop with all
new style choices (found under Preferences - Tool Bars).
The various styles give a polished look to the Commence
windows and tool bars by altering subtle details such as
the title bar colors, window borders, view bar and tool bar
background color, rounded edges vs square edges for
view windows, and other visual attributes. 
Choices
include Office 2007, Windows Vista, Windows Vista
(Black) for a monochrome look, Windows XP Luna, and
Windows XP Royal.
High resolution icons replace the previous command bar
buttons along the top of the Commence window (Drag &
Drop, Print, Design View, Sort View, etc.).
Number (0 - 9) icons have been added to the icon library
for use with view bar and tool bar buttons.
Existing view bar icons have all be converted to high
resolution full color images and standardized to 16x16 and
32x32 sizes for small and large icons respectively.
Custom high resolution icons stored in an external
directory can now be used as Agent tool bar buttons in
addition to saved view bar buttons.
Calendar
Calendar has been redesigned to echo the styling of the
Microsoft Outlook calendar.
More at-a-glance features provide information such as the
day of week above the time slots in Day format, and
showing the short date before each week for 2 Weeks,
Month, and 6 Weeks format.
Browse buttons now appear on dates with no appointment
to quickly view the date of your next or previous
appointment.
Expanded display area at top of day planner for
appointments with no specific start time
Calendars now support WYSIWYG printing.
Week numbers are now displayed near the mini months
on Calendar printouts.
Better drag & drop support for Calendars within multi-
views. Quickly reschedule appointments, extend meeting
times, and more.
Views
Multi-views now have the option of displaying the view
icon next to the tab name. Icons are selected in the Save
View As dialog when creating or modifying the secondary
view.
Color Code Template colors have been updated to offer
lighter background color choices while still retaining some
of the deeper colors which are the preferred choice for text
color coding.
Usability Enhancements
Natural date language now supports simplified phrases
such as 'beginning of next month', where 'the beginning of
next month' previously required.
More custom views can now be saved. Database capacity
has been increased from 1000 to 2000 for the maximum
number of views per database.
Calendar color coding has been turned off by default to
maintain expected view appearances in upgraded
databases. Please review your color code templates in
converted databases to verify the appropriate colors are
selected for foreground vs background use.
Miscellaneous
Calendar Design view settings have been updated to
support the redesigned calendar view options. Calendar
preferences dialog has also been updated to remove
obsolete options for day planner printing. Calendar Image
format and corresponding image packs are no longer
available.
Select Items window is no longer accessible by double-
clicking on a date header in calendars.
Improved detail form performance by resolving GDI
memory usage issue.
Improved multi-view performance with better caching.
Resolved application error when configuring workgroup
permissions dialog for more than 50 desktops and
categories.
Commence Outlook Address Book installation issue has
been resolved.

the error occurs. You can use this number to look up a message
in this list and select the number of the message below to see a
help topic on that message.
 
Note that you can press F1 when the error message is displayed
by Commence (i.e., when the error occurs) to see the same help
topic.

Keyboard Commands and Mouse Shortcuts
You can use Commence with a keyboard only, or a mouse and
keyboard. A mouse is recommended because it makes working in
the Microsoft Windows environment easier.
 
The following topics explain the keyboard equivalents for those
activities in Commence that do not require a mouse.
 
the keys you use to select menu commands
keys you use to fill out a dialog box or detail form
keys you can use to select from and scroll through a multiple
selection list box
keys you use to arrange fields on a detail form
keys you use to navigate around a view (in particular, the
key equivalents for the calendar view control panel)
window control keys
 
Two activities, however, cannot be performed without the aid of a
mouse:
 
choosing a view icon from the view bar
choosing a command icon from the tool bar
 
We also list some mouse shortcuts that you can use in many
dialog boxes within Commence.
Valid Field Codes or Keywords
See Also
General Commence keywords
Keyword
Evaluates To
(-ProgramDir-)
The full path to COMMENCE.EXE, e.g.,
c:\program files\commence
(-ProgramExe-)
The full path and filename of the
COMMENCE.EXE, e.g., c:\program
files\commence\commence.exe
(-AppDataDir-)
The full path to the Application Data
Directory where user-created files and
interactive files are stored by default (e.g.
backups, exports, etc.) e.g., c:\program
data\commence\commence\7.5 or
c:\documents and settings\all
users\application
data\commence\commence\7.5
(-
DatabasePath-)
The full path of the currently active
database.
(-
DatabaseName-)
The Name of the currently active database.
(-Category-)
The name of the current category.
 
The following keywords are supported in Filters.
 
Keyword
Evaluates To
(-Me-)
The item name selected in Preferences -
Personal Info (found under the Customize
menu or Edit menu depending on your
version of Commence)
(-Date-)
The current date. In a date type field, this is
equivalent to Today. In any other field, it is
equivalent to today’s date in long format,.e.g.
January 01, 2010.
(-Time-)
The current time. In a time type field, this is
equivalent to Now. In any other field, it is
equivalent to the current time, e.g. 1:34PM.

most recently active Calendar view. This is
used for View Linking and Multi-View filtering
(-EndDate-)

used for View Linking and Multi-View

Date field Is Between (-StartDate-) and (-
EndDate-)
(-

The selected date of the most recently active
Calendar view. This is used for View Linking
and Multi-View filtering.
(-

The highlighted item in the primary view.
This is used for View Linking and Multi-View
filtering.
(-
SelectedText-)
Search string for field(s) in the primary view.
This is used for Multi-View filtering.

Command Line Options
See Also
You can customize your Commence shortcut by
adding the following options to the end of the Target,
e.g., "c:\program files (x86)\commence\commence
rm workgroup\commence.exe" /PROMPT /TRACE.
Tips - To get started, right click on your Commence
shortcut and choose Properties. Click in the Target
box and use the END key on your keyboard to go to
the end of this line. Start typing your command line
options after the quote marks. You can add more than
one command line option; just put a space between
each one.
 
/QUIET
If you need to open more than one Commence database at the
same time, use this option to suppress the warning message that
there is another copy of Commence running. Commence will
quietly go ahead and start another instance.
 
/DATABASE=database name
Will start Commence and open the named database. Do not use
quote marks around the database name. Also note the forward
slash character is not supported in database names.
 
/DATABASE=database path
Will start Commence and open the database located in the
named directory.
 
/PROMPT
Will present a list of installed databases so you can select one to
open.
 
/NOVIEWS
Will not open any views when starting Commence. Note the View
bar icons will also be hidden temporarily. Switching to a different
desktop once in Commence will restore all views and view icons.
 
/NOAGENTS
Will prevent agent processing from occurring while Commence is
running. This is useful if you think you have an agent that is
causing problems with your database.
 
/NOTAPI
Will prevent Commence from loading and using TAPI. If
used,Commence will not be able to auto-
dial.
 
/TRACE
Will place additional information into the active.log file. The use of
this option may be requested by Commence Technical Support to
assist in troubleshooting.
 
/REINDEX
Will create new indices for all items when starting Commence.
Depending on the size of the database and the number of items,
this may take a while. If so, the PC should not be rebooted and
you should wait until the reindexing has completed.
