command. Choose the letter template you want to use for your
letter and choose the View button to edit the template; select the
Print button to print the letter. To log the letter, turn on the Log
check box and optionally type in a name for the log item.
 
The Send Letter dialog box contains these elements:
SEND LETTER TO
This option enables a letter to be sent to the selected item or to
all items in the active view.
TEMPLATES LIST BOX
This list box shows the names and Types of all templates
available for use with the active category. Select which letter you
want to send by highlighting one of the templates in the list.
 
Commence provides some initial templates, but you will probably
want to customize these or create new ones. The details of
setting up and using both text and formatted letter templates are
discussed in Customize-Database-Letter Template.
DESTINATION (Word 2000/XP)
This displays the possible destinations for the merged letter,
label, or envelope template. Select where the letter should be
sent after it has been merged. Current options include Printer,
FAX, Draft or E-Mail.
APPLICATIONS (Word 97)
This displays the word processor applications that were installed
when you installed Commence. If you have added any word
processor applications since Commence was installed, you will
need to re-install Commence.
When you create a letter, you can also choose to log it by turning
on the Log check box. Logging creates a letter log item that is
connected to the recipient and contains other information about
the letter. See your Event Logs Preference for details.
MERGE BUTTON
When the Preview button is checked, this button enables you to
type the body of your letter or to preview the letter and make
changes before sending it. Any field codes in the letter will be
filled in with the data from the item that was highlighted when you
chose Send Letter.
If you select a template with a Type of TXT (a plain text file) and
chose Merge in the dialog box, Commence launches your
preferred text word processor with the text file. At that point, you
can convert the letter into the native format of the word processor
(usually the word processor offers you this option when it reads in
the file.) From the word processor, you can edit the letter
(including adding whatever formatting is supported by the word
processor), print it, and/or save it as a normal word processor file.
If you select a formatted template (i.e., a file with a Type other
than TXT, such as DOC or SAM) and chose Merge in the dialog
box, Commence sends DDE messages to the word processor
instructing it to copy the template into a new document file and
then retrieve the needed data field values from Commence. (The
data field substitution is done by a macro running in the word
processor which communicates with Commence to get the
needed data values.) After the data values are substituted into the
document, Commence maximizes the word processor and you
can manually edit, print, or save the new letter.
 
When the Preview button is cleared, and you select a TXT
template and chose Merge, Commence creates a text letter by
replacing the Field Codes in the template with data values from
the highlighted item in the active view and displays the Print
Letter dialog box.
If you select a formatted template and chose Merge, Commence
sends DDE messages to the word processor instructing it to copy
the template into a new document file and then retrieve the
needed data field values from Commence. (The data field
substitution is done by a macro running in the word processor
which communicates with Commence to get the needed data
values.) After the data values are substituted into the document,
the word processor prints the document and closes the file. Note
that the printed document will be formatted with whatever
formatting you have specified in the template file.
If you want to cancel printing a formatted letter, you must first
interrupt the word processor macro and then cancel the print job.
How you cancel a macro depends on the macro language of the
word processor; pressing the ESC key cancels a macro in Word
for Windows and WordPerfect.
The letter prints on the default printer selected for the word
processor.
Note: If you want to specify other print options for a formatted
letter (e.g., print multiple copies, print a subsection of the letter
etc.) use the Preview option to create the letter, and then use the
word processor directly to enter the options.
PREVIEW
This option enables the merged letter template to be viewed
before sending it to its destination.
INCLUDE MERGED DOCUMENT WITH LOG ITEM
This option enables the document containing the merged letter
template to be saved with the Log item stored in Commence.
When this option is cleared, only the Log item is created, without
the actual document.
CUSTOM LETTER FILENAME
You can type a full path or file name for a letter in this text box. If
you enter a full path into the Custom Letter Filename text box,
then Commence saves the letter in the location specified by the
path. If you enter only a file name, then the letter is saved to a
default directory specified via Edit-Preferences-Letters.
--
CANCEL BUTTON
Select the Cancel button to return to the work area.
Customize-Database-Letter Template
See Also
Use this command to display the Letter Template tab, from which
you can add templates, edit existing templates, and delete
templates. Commence provides some initial templates, but you
will probably want to customize these or create new ones.
 
Letter templates are used as the guideline for creating form
letters. Letter templates can contain ordinary text (the body of the
letter) as well as Field Codes that represent field values from
items in a given category in Commence (like the
Person/Company category). Each letter template is associated
with a specific category, and can only be used to send letters to
items in that category. All templates are word processor files that
are stored in the tmplts subdirectory of your active database
directory. Commence letter template filenames always start with
the letters PIM followed by some numbers, and have an
extension of .TXT or whatever is appropriate for your word
processor.
 
Commence supports two types of letter templates, text templates
and formatted templates. You can use text templates with or
without an external word processor. Text templates produce ASCII
text letters, which are useful for transmitting letters electronically
or for simple memos. Text templates cannot contain graphics or
formatted text (i.e. bold, italic, etc.)
 
Formatted templates are more customizable and can take
advantage of the word processor features which allow you to use
graphics and different fonts in your letter template. Formatted
letter templates can only be used if you have a word processor
that has a macro language and supports both client and server
modes of DDE (Dynamic Data Exchange). Commence currently
provides macros for three such word processors: Microsoft Word
and WordPerfect for Windows.
Select Telephone Number Dialog Box
This dialog box, displayed when you select the Dial button, allows
you to choose from among multiple telephone numbers displayed
in an open view or item detail.
 
The list box shows the field labels of the telephone fields in the
active view or item detail. Highlight one of the numbers and select
the Dial button to have your modem dial the number contained in
the named field.
 
Shortcut: Double-click the left mouse button while pointing at one
of the field labels to dial the number without selecting the Dial
button.
 
Once you select the number to dial, the Commence Dialing dialog
box is displayed.
 
Select the Cancel button to return to the active view or item detail
without dialing a number.
Commence Dialing Dialog Box
See Also
This dialog box displays the phone number that is being dialed.
Depending on how you set your dialing preferences (see Dialer
Preferences), this phone number may include access codes as
well as the number in the telephone field.
 
Selecting the Change Options button allows you to change the
dialing options via TAPI.
 
You can pick the phone up at any point after it has dialed. Select
the Talk button in the ATSP Call Status dialog box to start the
phone conversation with the other party.
 
Selecting the Hang Up button in the ATSP Call Status dialog box
allows you to terminate the dialing by disconnecting the modem.
Configuring MS Outlook Address Book
This dialog box is displayed when you select the Edit-
Preferences-Other Apps command.
 
Microsoft Outlook is an electronic mail package included with
Windows. Multiple MAPI compliant mail systems can plug into MS
Outlook in order to provide a single, consistent interface for
electronic mail. Since MS Outlook operates as a front–end for
multiple and possibly very different mail systems, there are some
associated inherent problems. As an example, each mail system
typically has its own address book, which leads to difficultly in
locating an individual’s address, as well as potential redundant
data.
 
Commence solves this problem by providing its users with a
universal MAPI–compliant address book. A specified category
within Commence may now be accessed by Microsoft Outlook for
use as an Address Book for such applications as addressing
electronic mail messages, faxing documents, etc. This eliminates
the need to enter duplicate information into both Commence and
Outlook. To use this option:
 
1. From the Configure Commence as: combo box, select
Microsoft Outlook address book.
2. Select the category you want to use as an address book.
Usually, this is the Person category.
3. In order for MS Outlook to access the correct address
information within a Commence category, the fields used by
Outlook must be mapped to the fields in the Commence
category. Double click on the desired MS Outlook Type field
on the left side of the form, or highlight the desired MS
Outlook Type field on the left and select Edit.
4. Select the corresponding Commence Field from the Combo
Box. Select OK when done.
5. Repeat steps 3 and 4 for all remaining MS Outlook Type
fields that are to map to Commence Fields. Only those fields
that will be used need to be mapped.
6. When all MS Outlook Type fields have been mapped to
Commence Fields, select Done to save the configuration.
 
Performing the steps below will add the Commence Address
Book to the Microsoft Outlook Profile.
7. Run the Windows Control Panel.
8. Double click on the Mail and Fax icon. The MS Outlook
Settings Properties dialog box will be displayed. Select the
Services tab.
9. Select the Add button. A list of available services will be
displayed.
10. Highlight the Commence Address Book selection. Select
OK to add the Commence Address Book to the Outlook
profile.
 
Performing the steps below will configure the default Microsoft
Outlook Address Book to be the Commence Address Book.
11. The MS Outlook Settings Properties dialog box should
still be displayed. (If not, repeat steps 7 and 8 above.) Select
the Addressing Tab.
12. From the Show this address list first: pull–down menu,
select Commence Address Book.
13. From the Keep personal addresses in: pull–down menu,
select Commence Address Book.
14. Click on the Add button.
15. Highlight Commence Address Book and click on the Add
button.
16. Click on Close.
17. The Commence Address Book is now shown in the list of
Address Books for the When sending mail, check names
using these address lists in the following order: option.
18. Move the Commence Address Book to the top of the list.
19. Select OK.
20. Select OK again, and then close the Windows Control
Panel
Microsoft Outlook is now configured to use the information
maintained within Commence as an Address Book, called
Commence Address Book.
Print Setup Dialog Box
This dialog box is displayed by the Print Setup command or by
the Printer button in the Add/Edit Paper Layout dialog box.
This command allows you to setup your printers.
 
 
 
 
 
Menu Pick Trigger
A Menu Pick trigger
is manually controlled: it fires when you select the menu pick
associated with the trigger. Menu Pick triggers are added as new
menu entries on the Home
menu. You enter the text of the menu item in the Menu Text text
box.
You can also enter Help Text that is associated with the entry
(when the highlight is over the menu pick, the help text appears
on the status bar).
The help text is useful to remind you of the agent's
purpose. You can also define which desktop
the agent will appear and a keyboard hotkey.
 
A Menu Pick trigger can be associated with a specific category
(e.g., Person/Company) using the Active Category drop-down
list box.
If you make this association, then the menu item is only enabled
when
either a view of the category or an item detail from that category
is
active. If another category is active, the menu item is grayed out.
If
you check All Categories, then
the menu pick can always be selected, regardless of the active
category.
 
A Menu Pick trigger that is
associated with a category passes data from a single item
in that category as trigger data. This trigger item is either the
selected data item in a view, or the data item from the displayed
item detail
that has the focus.
 
 
 
 
Agent Tab
This tab is displayed when you select the Customize-Database-
Agent command.
 
This tab lets you manage the agents provided with Commence
and define and manage your own agents. You can:
 
Select the Add button to add a new agent.
Highlight an agent name in the Agent Names list box and
select one of the Edit, Copy, or Delete buttons to edit, copy,
or delete the highlighted agent.
 
The elements of this dialog box are:
AGENT NAMES LIST BOX
The Agent Names list box shows you all the agents that are
currently defined. The status of each agent is displayed to the
right of the agent name. An agent status can either be active or
inactive.
 
When an agent is highlighted, the Agent Description message
box will show a summary of the agent’s trigger, conditions, and
actions.
ADD BUTTON
To add a new agent, select the Add button. Commence displays
the New Agent dialog box.
EDIT BUTTON
To examine or edit an agent, highlight it and select the Edit
button. This brings up the details of the agent in the Edit Agent
dialog box.
 
Shortcut: Double-click the left mouse button while pointing at an
agent name in the Agents list box to edit the agent's definition
without selecting the Edit button.
COPY BUTTON
To make a copy of an existing agent and edit the copy, highlight
the agent and select the Copy button. The details of the copied
agent are displayed in the New Agent dialog box.
DELETE BUTTON
To delete an agent, highlight it in the Agents list box and select
the Delete button. Commence asks for confirmation before
deleting the highlighted agent.
OK BUTTON
Once you are finished working with agents, select the OK button
to return to the Agent tab.
Copy To File Dialog Box
This dialog box is displayed by the Edit-Copy To command.
 
Type a file name into the text box and select OK. Select Cancel
to cancel the copy to file operation.
 
If the file name you supply is an existing file, Commence asks you
if you want to overwrite the file; if you select Yes, the highlighted
text is written to the file and the existing contents of the file are
lost. Select No and you are returned to this dialog box.
Paste From File Dialog Box
This dialog box is displayed by the Edit-Paste From command.
The Filename text box displays the default wild card string used
to selectively list the contents of the drive or folder named in the
Save in list box. The files in this drive or folder are listed in the
box.
Change the contents of the Filename box and the drive or folder
name to examine filenames on your system. Do this by:
Typing a filename or wildcard string into the Filename text box
and pressing the ENTER key; this changes the file list in the
box.
Selecting a drive or folder name in the Save in list box; this
changes the directory whose files are listed in the box.
Once you find the filename that you want, select it from the list;
the filename is moved to the Filename box. Select OK to confirm
your selection. Select Cancel to cancel the paste from file
operation.
Select Search Categories Dialog Box
This dialog box is displayed when you select the Categories
button on the Global Search dialog box.
 
To use it, select and deselect categories for the search by clicking
the left mouse button over the name of a category in the list box
(or use the arrow keys and the space bar). Select OK after all of
the categories that you want to search are highlighted in the list
box.
 
The Select Search Categories dialog box contains these
elements:
SELECT ALL
Use the Select All button to highlight all of the categories in the
list.
DESELECT ALL
Use the Deselect All button to remove the highlight from all
categories.
ACTIVE CATEGORIES
Use the Active Categories button to search only the categories
that have a view open on the active desktop.
OK
Select the OK button to return to the Global Search dialog box
with the new list of categories to search.
CANCEL
Select the Cancel button to return to the Global Search dialog
box without changing the list of categories to search.
 
Note: On the first search after selecting the Global Search
command, all categories are selected by default.
Customize-Design View-Fields Tab
See Also
All views allow you to display different fields. The Report and Grid
type allows you to display any fields in the category along with
any fields from connected categories. The Book type allows you
to display up to 20 fields in the category, along with the Name
field from connected categories. The Gantt view displays the
Name field and the clarify field, and uses the date fields in the
category to display a time line. You can select any two dates in
the category to be used for the start and end of the time line. For
the Calendar view, up to five fields can be displayed. You can
select any Date and up to two time fields to be used for displaying
the items on the Calendar.
Time Conflict
This button is found on the Customize - Database - Category tab.
When you press the Time Conflict button, Commence displays
the Time Conflict Layout dialog box.
 
The Time Conflict Layout dialog box lets you:
 
-- select the fields to be used for time conflict checking for the
selected category
-- enable time conflict checking for the selected category.
-- specify a connection to use when checking for time conflicts.
 
Enabling time conflict checking for a category means that
Commence will check each item added to the category for a time
conflict with items already in the category. If a conflict is detected,
Commence asks you if you want to add the conflicting item
anyway.
 
For example, the Calendar category has time conflict enabled. If
you attempt to add more than one item occurring at the same
time on the same day, a warning message is displayed before the
new item is added. You can choose to abort or continue the
addition of the conflicting item. (Note that time slots with multiple
items display the word (multiple) instead of the item names.)
 
Selecting fields for time conflict checking will also let Commence
warn you if you specify a start time for an item that is later than
the specified end time.
Print Calendar Dialog Box
When you select the File-Print command from the menu bar with
an active calendar view, or select a Print Items agent action from
the Add/Edit Action dialog box with a calendar view selected,
Commence displays the Print Calendar dialog box.
PRINT TITLE
The Print Title text box displays the default title for printing the
view. The default title is either the title you specified using the
Customize-Design View-Display command or (if you have not
specified your own default) the name of the category used by the
view.
 
You can edit the title that appears in the Title text box to change
the title for this printing of the view only. Whether or not the title
is actually printed with the view depends on the option set using
the File-Page Setup command. The Print Title is unavailable for
the Day Planner.
STYLE
Choose the calendar style to use when printing the view by
selecting one of the radio buttons in the Style radio group. The
items printed in each style are determined by the From and To
dates (see below). This radio group is unavailable if the Detail
Only button is selected. The calendar style options are:
 
Day List
Prints three monthly calendars (previous, present, and next
months) at the top of the page, with a listing of items
underneath.
 
Day Planner
Prints the date and three months of monthly calendars at the
top of the page; with a day planner style calendar of items
underneath. You can display items for a range of 9 or 12 hours
by selecting the appropriate radio button. Select the start time
of the hour range from the list box. The start time must be
between midnight and 3:00PM.
 
Week List
Prints a week's items in a grid of 7 days per page.
 
Week Planner
Prints the week and three months of monthly calendars at the
top of the page; with the week’s items on the page under each
day of the week
 
Month Planner
Prints a month's items in a monthly calendar grid; each item is
printed in the box for the day on which it occurs.
 
6-Week Planner
Prints six weeks of items in a six-week calendar grid; each item
is printed in the box for the day on which it occurs.
 
FROM
The From text box specifies the start date for printing items from
a calendar view. Only items in the range specified by the From
and To dates are printed. Commence assumes the calendar
view's active date as the From date for Line and Day Planner
styles, and the beginning of the week or month for Week and
Month Planner styles, respectively. Commence will change the
From date accordingly as you choose different style options. You
can change this date by editing the date in the From text box.
TO
The To text box specifies the end date for printing items from a
calendar view. Only items in the range specified by the From and
To dates are printed. Commence assumes the calendar view's
active date as the To date for Line and Day Planner styles; the
end of the week or month for Week and Planner styles,
respectively. Commence will change the To date accordingly as
you choose different style options. You can change this date by
editing the date in the To text box.
OK
Select OK to return to the Print dialog box.
CANCEL
Select Cancel to cancel the print options.
Calendar Preferences Dialog Box
This dialog box is displayed when you choose the Edit-
Preferences-Calendar command.
Use the Week Start Day drop down list box to select the day you
want to use as the start of a week. Wherever possible, the same
starting day will also be used to display the mini-month calendars.
Note: The Week Planner mode of the calendar uses a combined
Saturday/Sunday block and therefore cannot be displayed with a
Sunday start day.
Database Preferences Dialog Box
This dialog box is displayed when you choose the Edit-
Preferences-Database command.
DATABASE PASSWORD
Turn on the Password Protect Database check box if you wish
to restrict who can access the currently open database. If
selected, Commence will prompt for a password whenever you
open this database.
To set (or change) your password, select the Set Password
button. The Change Database Password dialog box will be
displayed. For more information see Database Protection.
SET SHARED CHECKBOX BY DEFAULT
This option allows you to select whether data added to a shared
database is automatically marked as shared. If you usually add
shared data, then you should select this checkbox. If you usually
add private data, then clear the checkbox.
This option applies to the shared toolbar button on detail forms,
and to the Shared checkbox in the Add and Edit dialogs for the
definitions that can be shared in a database. This option is used
only to set the initial state of the Shared button, and you can
always override this setting by setting or clearing the Shared
button and then saving the data.
Note: If you are not authorized to update items in a category, the
Shared check box will be grayed out.
SYNCHRONIZE DATA FILES
This option allows you to select whether data files referenced by
shared items are automatically copied along with the shared
items. If this option is cleared, data files will not be copied from
this database to the server database. If this option is selected,
which is the default, data files will be copied from this database to
the server database and will be received from other client
databases.
This option can be cleared if data files referenced by shared
items are already located in a shared directory on the network.
The file name and path referenced in the shared item are
changed to reflect the path selected for the External Data Files
directory. You will need to change this directory to reflect the path
of the shared directory by selecting Edit-Preferences-Other.
This option should be selected if you do not store data files in a
shared network directory. Selecting this option will cause
Commence to copy the data files associated with shared items so
that they are available on every client database. Commence will
also automatically update the data file path to reflect the file’s
location on the client workstation, and will attempt to preserve the
filename if possible.
Note: This option must be selected at the server database to
enable clients to synchronize data files. Disabling this option
at the server will disable it for the shared database, even if
the option is selected at the client databases.
Event Logs Preferences Dialog Box
This dialog box is displayed when you choose the Edit-
Preferences-Event Logs command.
LOG PHONE CALLS TO
The Log Phone Calls to group box lets you select the category
to be used as the log category when you log incoming or outgoing
phone calls. The default category is Journal, which contains
fields that will time your phone call.
 
When choosing an alternate category for the letter or phone log,
you should choose a category similar to the defaults given above
so that Commence can keep proper track of your logged phone
calls and letters.
 
For example, the default phone log has default time entries that
allow Commence to place accurate start and end times in the log,
and figure the duration of the phone call.
ADD LOG ENTRY FOR OUTGOING CALL IF...
If turn on the Phone Call is answered checkbox, Commence will
log any outgoing phone calls which were automatically dialed by
Commence, only if the phone call is answered by the other party.
If a busy signal is detected, or the phone call does not complete
properly for any reason, the call will not be logged.
 
If you need to log all phone calls, even if you do not connect with
the other party, turn on the Phone Call is not answered
checkbox. The attempted phone call will be logged, even if
Commence receives a busy signal or for some reason is unable
to complete the phone call.
LOG LETTERS TO
The Log Letters to group box lets you select the default category
to be used as the log category when you use the Tools-Send
Letter dialog boxes. The default category is Notes/History.
 
When choosing an alternate category for the letter or phone log,
you should choose a category similar to the defaults given above
so that Commence can keep proper track of your logged phone
calls and letters.
 
For example, the default letter log has a data file field that points
to the letter sent. You can view the letter directly from the item
detail of the letter log item; if you choose a category with no data
file field, you will not be able to view a letter from its letter log
item. Use the Customize-Database-Category command to look at
the definition of the default letter and phone log categories.
SAVE IN DIRECTORY
The Save in Directory section determines where Commence
saves the letters that you log. By default, this is in a subdirectory
called \letters in your Commence program directory. If you want
to change this selection, type the name of the new directory in the
Save in Directory text box. You can also use the Select button to
browse your hard disk to find a suitable directory. This displays
the Select Directory dialog box. After you choose a directory path
and select OK, the Event Logs Preferences dialog box appears
with the chosen directory shown in the Directory text box.
 
Note that the Save In Directory is not the directory where your
letter templates are stored. Commence will always store your
letter templates in the \tmplts subdirectory of your current
database.
Meeting Preferences Dialog Box
This dialog box is displayed when you choose the Edit-
Preferences-Meeting command.
 
The Meeting Preferences dialog box contains nine drop-down
list boxes.
MEETING CATEGORY
Select the category for which the Schedule Meetings command
will add items. The Appointment category is the default for the
databases shipped with Commence. This is a required field.
DATE FIELD
Select a field in the Meeting Category to use for the meeting date.
Date is the default for the databases shipped with Commence.
This is a required field.
START TIME FIELD
Select a field in the Meeting Category to use for the meeting start
time. Start Time is the default in the databases shipped with
Commence. This is a required field.
END TIME FIELD
Select a field in the Meeting Category to use for the meeting end
time. End Time is the default in the databases shipped with
Commence. This is a required field.
COMMENTS FIELD
Select a field in the Meeting Category to use for the agenda,
comments, or notes for a meeting. This field is optional.
PARTICIPANTS CONNECTION
Select a connection definition to use when connecting a
Participant to the meeting. This field is optional but highly
recommended.
LOCATION CONNECTION
Select a connection definition to use when connecting the
selected Located-In item to the meeting. This field is optional.
COORDINATOR CONNECTION
Select a connection definition to use when connecting the
selected Coordinated-By item to the meeting. This field is
optional.
RESOURCES CONNECTION
Select a connection definition to use when connecting the items
selected in the Resources dialog box to the meeting. This field is
optional.
DONE
Select this button to confirm your choices for Meeting
Preferences and return to the work area.
CANCEL
Select this button to cancel the currently specified Meeting
Preferences and return to the work area without making any
changes.
Other Preferences Dialog Box
This dialog box is displayed when you choose the Edit-
Preferences-Other command.
 
ITEM DETAIL
Use the Treat 'Enter' Like 'Tab' Key option to change how the
enter key operates on a detail form. If it is checked, the enter key
will move you to the next field just as the tab key does. If it is not
checked, the enter key will invoke the default button, that is, the
button with the dark line around it. The sole exception is that if
you are in a text field with more than one line, the enter key will
insert a new line character regardless of how this preference is
set.
 
If the Confirm Item Deletes option is checked, Commence will
prompt you for confirmation whenever you select Edit-Delete
Item. If not checked, Commence will delete the item from your
category without asking for confirmation.
 
If the Disable 'Save and add another' option is checked,
Commence will disable the button on the Item Detail toolbar that
opens a new blank form upon saving the current item.
 
AGENTS / OUTLOOK SYNC
The Agent options determine how Commence handles message
boxes, questions boxes, and error messages displayed by
Commence during an agent's execution.
Prompt for response to questions
Automatically answer questions (this is the default)
Automatically answer questions causes Commence to
affirmatively answer interrogative boxes with Yes or OK as a
response, whichever applies (except for message boxes
displayed by Message Box agent actions). The text of the box is
copied to the Message log so that you can tell when a
Commence agent has automatically answered questions for you.
 
The advantage of the Automatically answer questions option is
that it allows you to run agents while your PC is unattended. The
disadvantage is that it answers all questions affirmatively, and this
may not always be what you want.
 
If you turn on the Prompt for response to questions radio
button, then Commence will always prompt for an answer and will
wait for a response.
The advantage of the Prompt for response to questions option
is that it gives you the chance to respond to individual prompts
appropriately. The disadvantage is that agent message boxes will
wait an infinite amount of time for your response, and this may
mean that required processing of your data is not performed as
needed.
 
Note: The exceptions are noted below. These will always wait an
infinite amount of time for your response, and are unaffected by
the setting of this preference.
Message boxes displayed by Message Box agent actions
The message to insert another diskette when Backup runs
out of disk space
 
The option to Disable Outlook Sync Feature prevents the
unintentional use of the Outlook Synchronization feature. This
gives you the ability to pre-configure the mappings for Outlook /
Windows Mobile Calendar, Contacts, Tasks, and Notes and to
have control over when and if that feature will be accessible to
users.
 
This option is turned off by default. When the option Disable
Outlook Sync Feature is enabled, the toolbar menu command to
synchronize with Outlook becomes inactive. Agent actions
designed to auto-synchronize with Outlook are also disabled with
this option, however the agent active/inactive status remains
unchanged.
NAME FIELDS
If the Reverse "Last, First" check box is checked, whenever
Commence is printing letters or exporting it will change any name
field containing a comma to "First Last" name. If this check box is
not checked, Commence will not reverse the contents of the
name field in these situations.
SHORT DATE RANGE
Use the Short Date Range preference to control how you want
Commence to expand a two-digit year that you enter into a date
field. For example, if you enter a date such as "1/4/29" and this
option is set to the default, the years 1930-2029, Commence
assumes you mean "1/4/2029". If you changed the Short Date
Range to be 1920-2019 and re-entered "1/4/29", Commence
would expand it to "1/4/1929". Commence lets you enter dates
from January 1, 1905 to December 31, 2083.
EXTERNAL DATA FILES
When you transfer in a file from another database that has related
data files (such as logged letters), Commence needs to know
where to put the related data files on your hard disk. This is
because the directory structure on your computer and the
directory structure of the computer on which the transfer file was
created are most likely different.
 
The currently selected directory is displayed in the Directory box.
If you check the Overwrite existing file when file names
conflict option, then new files that are transferred in will be
overwritten if the filename matches one that already exists. If the
option is turned off, then files already in the directory with the
same names as incoming files are retained and the incoming files
are not added.
 
If you want to change the current data file directory, type the
name of the directory in the Directory text box, or use the Select
button to find a suitable directory. This displays the Select
Directory dialog box.
 
After you choose a directory path and select OK, the Other
Preferences dialog box appears with the chosen directory shown
in the Directory text box.
Letter Fax Macros (Word 97, WordPerfect, AmiPro)
See Also
Commence supports macros for WinFax Pro. The macros, along
with one of the word processors that works with the Commence
Letters facility, allow you to fax a letter instead of printing it.
 
These macros retrieve the selected recipient’s fax number from
Commence, select your fax printer, send the fax number to the
fax software, fax the selected letter(s), and then re-select your
default printer. The macros retrieve the fax number by looking for
a telephone number field that contains “fax” or FAX” in its name.
You can decide whether you want to print or fax on a per-letter
basis.
 
Because the macros send the fax number to the Fax software,
the fax is sent without you having to interact with the Fax
software. If you select Tools-Send Letter, letters can be created
and faxed for every item from a view in Commence. This is useful
for sending a batch of customized faxes to a group of recipients.
Batch faxes cannot be deferred to be sent at a later time.
Letter Preferences Dialog Box
This dialog box is displayed when you choose the Edit-
Preferences-Letters command.
TEXT LETTERS
The section entitled Text Letters is concerned with your selection
of a word processor to use for letters created with text templates.
The default word processor is Write, but you can select any word
processor that is capable of reading in and saving flat ASCII text
files.
 
Enter the word processor in the text box to the right of Program
Filename. If the program is in your path, you can just enter the
program filename, otherwise enter the complete path. You can
also use the Select button to the right of Program Filename to
browse your PC file system to find a program.
 
Enter the file extension that your word processor uses for its
native files in the File Extension text box. When Commence
creates a text letter file (not a template file), it will use this
extension if you have requested to view the new letter in your
word processor.
Note: If you are using Ami Pro for Windows you must enter TXT
not SAM since Ami Pro will not read in a text file unless it has this
extension.)
FORMATTED LETTERS
This is used to set your preferences for formatted letter
templates. Highlight your word processor from the Word
Processor list box. Note that as you change your selection, the
entries for the fields in the Formatted Letters section change.
ALWAYS PREVIEW
This is used to set the default value for the Preview option on the
Send Letter dialog.
Open/Manage Views Dialog Box
The Open/Manage Views dialog box is displayed when you
select the View-Open/Manage Views command. Use it to open,
edit or delete a saved view or create a new one. For more
information on creating a new view, see New View Dialog Box
 
The elements of this dialog box are:
SORT BY
The radio button selected from this group determines whether the
list of views is sorted by view name, category name, or view type.
Select the radio button next to the sorting method you prefer, and
the list is re-sorted automatically.
LIST OF VIEWS
The list of all saved views is displayed in a list box; use the scroll
bar at right to scroll through the list. Highlight the name of the
view you want to display.
Shortcut: You can open a view by double-clicking the mouse on
the view name.
OPEN
Once you've highlighted the view you want to open from the view
list box, select the Open button to display the view. The saved
view is displayed and becomes the active view.
 
CREATE NEW
This button lets you add a new view for any category, using any
view type. For more information on creating a new view, see New
View Dialog Box.
EDIT
If you select the Edit button, the Edit View dialog box is
displayed. This box allows you to change the view name and
location.
DELETE
Once you've highlighted the view you want to delete from the
view list box, select the Delete button to delete the view.
DONE
Use the Done button to cancel the View-Open/Manage Views
command.
Delete View Dialog Box
This dialog box is displayed when you select the Delete button on
the View-Open/Manage Views tab. The message asks you to
confirm the deletion of the saved view.
 
If you select the Yes button, the selected view is deleted from
Commence.
 
Note: Deleting a view does not delete the data contained within
that view. If you delete the last view for a category, Commence
will prompt you to delete the category. You should select No,
unless you wish to delete all data and associated definitions
which may depend on this category.
Formatting a custom .HTX file for use with
Commence
You can override the default HTML templates used by
Commence. To do so, create a set of .HTX files with specific
filenames listed below and store these files in the \TMPLTS
subdirectory beneath the database directory. The templates
names are:
CALHTML.HTX This template is used when the active view type
is a Calendar. The keyword <%htmlcalendar%> found in this
template is replaced with data from the calendar view in
Commence.
 
REPTHTML.HTX This template is used when the active view type
is a Report. The keyword <%htmlview%> found in this template is
replaced with data from the report view in Commence. The data
is presented in a horizontal table format in the HTML file
regardless of the view’s layout in Commence. The items in the
view will continue to be filtered and sorted in the HTML file as
they appeared in the Commence view. The order of fields in the
report is also maintained in the HTML file. Fonts and colors used
in the view are not translated to the HTML file.
 
You may use any HTML editor to create new templates then save
the completed templates using the filenames above. Alternatively,
you may modify the built-in templates shipped with Commence.
These templates are available when you install the Customer
Relationship Management database. Open this database and
determine the path to the data directory using Help - System
Information. The templates will be located in the \TMPLTS
directory below this path. Copy the .HTX files from this directory
to the \TMPLTS directory beneath your database directory.
 
Note: The default templates listed above contain important
keywords used by Commence to merge the active view data into
the HTML file. Do not remove these keywords from the HTX files.
Save As HTML Dialog Box
The Save As HTML wizard steps you through the creation of an
HTML file containing a table of all the items in the active view.
The number of columns in the table will match the number of
fields and connections displayed in the view. The table will
automatically size itself to contain as many rows as needed to
include all items that were displayed in the view. If filters are
defined in the view, only those items that meet the filter will be
included in the table. The items will be sorted in the same order
as they appeared in the view. The resulting table will be saved in
an HTML file.
 
In the first step you will be prompted to make a choice:
Should Commence create a basic HTML document
containing the view information?
Should the active view be inserted into an existing formatted
HTML page?
 
See the corresponding sections below to determine which choice
is best for you.
By creating an HTML document containing the view
information When you select the first option the active
view is inserted into a basic HTML template provided by
Commence. Note that a very simple page is created when
using these built-in templates. You can override the default
templates used by Commence by saving a set of .HTX files
with specific filenames in the \TMPLTS directory beneath
your database directory. For additional information, see the
topic titled Formatting a custom .HTX file for use with
Commence.
 
After you select the first option and click Next>, you will be
prompted to enter some additional information about the view.
Feel free to enter values for any and all of the options below, or
you may leave them all blank.
Title: Any text you type here will appear at the top of the
HTML page. Typically, a brief description or Title of the active
view should be entered here.
Header: Any text you enter here will appear centered on the
line just above the table containing the items from the active
view.
Footer: Any text you enter here will be appear centered on
the line just below the table containing the items from the
active view.
By inserting the view information into an existing
formatted HTML page If you would prefer to use a custom
HTML template instead of the default templates included
with Commence, you may select the second option then
click Next> to continue. You will be prompted to enter the
filename of any HTML file or template of your choice. The
only requirement is that the HTML file you select must
already contain a special keyword in the position where
you want the table of data to appear. For additional
information, see the topic titled Formatting a custom .HTX
file for use with Commence. This option provides greater
flexibility by not requiring a specific .HTX template to be
used as with the first option.
Select the filename for the HTML document to be created In
the final step, you will be prompted to enter a new filename
to store the finished HTML document. You may use the
Browse button to select the directory where the file will be
stored. If you do not enter a path or change directories
then the file will be saved in the Commence program
directory. Type any filename followed by an extension of
.HTM then select the Finish button to save the file.
Select Window Dialog Box
See Also
Select a view to become the active view. This is useful when you
have many views displayed and some are hidden behind others.
 
Note that you can also use the key combination CTRL+F6 to
successively select views in the workspace as the active view.

This list box displays the names of all the currently open views.
